# Forage Catalog

**Inventory:** 1,000 fictional products  
**Source of truth:** `catalog.json`  

Human-readable merchandise workbook generated from the JSON catalog.

## Moto

### Juniper Short ADV Pant
`p0001` · **Ƶ 189** · ★ 4.5 (852 reviews) · Stock 24  
⬜ Photography needed  
A true short-inseam adventure pant rather than a full-length pant pretending a cuff solves everything. The 29-inch cut keeps the knee armor where it belongs, with stretch through the seat and inner leg for standing on the pegs. A removable waterproof liner and zip thigh vents make it useful from cool pavement mornings to warm gravel afternoons.  
**Material:** 600D abrasion-resistant textile with stretch panels · **Protection:** CE Level 2 knee armor; hip armor pockets · **Inseam:** 29 in Short · **Weather:** Removable waterproof liner; thigh vents · **Fit:** Women’s petite/short ADV fit  
**Options:** XS Short, S Short, M Short, L Short, XL Short  

### Switchgrass Mesh ADV Pant
`p0013` · **Ƶ 169** · ★ 3.7 (654 reviews) · Stock 19  
⬜ Photography needed  
Hot-weather riding pants built around airflow, not wishful thinking. Large mesh zones move air at road speed while reinforced seat and impact areas add abrasion resistance. The short cut and vertically adjustable knee pockets are the important bits: armor can actually land on a petite rider’s knees.  
**Material:** High-tenacity mesh with 600D reinforcement · **Protection:** CE Level 2 adjustable knee armor; CE Level 1 hips · **Inseam:** 28.5 in Short · **Weather:** Maximum-flow mesh; no waterproof liner · **Fit:** Women’s short summer ADV fit  
**Options:** XS Short, S Short, M Short, L Short  

### Ridgeline Over-Boot Trail Pant
`p0025` · **Ƶ 149** · ★ 4.0 (1,103 reviews) · Stock 26  
⬜ Photography needed  
A light over-boot trail pant for wearing over an armored base layer. Stretch ripstop keeps it mobile, abrasion overlays protect the usual contact points, and the lower leg opens wide enough for MX boots. No redundant armor is included—this one assumes protection is already underneath.  
**Material:** Stretch ripstop with abrasion overlays · **Protection:** Designed for armored base layers; no included armor · **Inseam:** 29 in · **Weather:** DWR finish; laser-cut vents · **Fit:** Relaxed over-boot trail cut  
**Options:** XS Short, S Short, M Short, L Short  

### Cloudbreak Laminated ADV Pant
`p0037` · **Ƶ 239** · ★ 4.8 (1,680 reviews) · Stock 31  
⬜ Photography needed  
The rainy-day option: waterproofing is laminated to the shell, so there is no liner to stop and install when the sky changes its mind. Direct thigh vents handle warmer stretches, and Level 2 armor is included at both knees and hips. The short pattern is cut as a short from the start.  
**Material:** 2-layer laminated waterproof textile · **Protection:** CE Level 2 knees and hips · **Inseam:** 29 in Short · **Weather:** Waterproof laminated shell; direct vents · **Fit:** Women’s short touring/ADV fit  
**Options:** XS Short, S Short, M Short, L Short  

### Red Clay Trail Jacket
`p0049` · **Ƶ 229** · ★ 4.3 (1,477 reviews) · Stock 30  
⬜ Photography needed  
A three-season textile jacket that leaves enough room to move without feeling like borrowed expedition gear. Stretch shoulders help when standing on the pegs, while large vents and a removable waterproof liner cover a wide weather range. Waist adjustment lets the jacket fit rather than simply hang.  
**Material:** 600D textile with stretch shoulders · **Protection:** CE Level 2 shoulder/elbow armor; back protector pocket · **Weather:** Removable waterproof liner; chest/back vents · **Fit:** Women’s ADV cut with waist adjustment · **Pockets:** 6 exterior, 2 interior  
**Options:** XS, S, M, L, XL  

### Blue Hour Mesh Jacket
`p0061` · **Ƶ 199** · ★ 4.4 (512 reviews) · Stock 18  
⬜ Photography needed  
For the day when a solid textile jacket feels like punishment. High-flow mesh covers the torso and inner arms, with denser textile at impact zones. The body is intentionally a little shorter so the hem does not bunch at the hips on a smaller rider.  
**Material:** High-tenacity mesh and 600D impact panels · **Protection:** CE Level 2 shoulders/elbows; back protector pocket · **Weather:** Hot-weather mesh shell · **Fit:** Women’s short-torso friendly cut · **Pockets:** 4 exterior, 2 interior  
**Options:** XS, S, M, L  

### Switchback Softshell Trail Jacket
`p0073` · **Ƶ 179** · ★ 4.5 (38 reviews) · Stock 5  
⬜ Photography needed  
A lighter trail shell for riders who already own separate armor. It blocks wind on faster connectors, sheds a brief shower, and packs down far smaller than a full ADV jacket. The stretch fabric is especially comfortable for repeated sit-to-stand riding.  
**Material:** Abrasion-resistant stretch softshell · **Protection:** Armor-ready; shoulder/elbow pockets · **Weather:** Wind resistant; DWR; large pit vents · **Fit:** Active trail fit · **Weight:** Lightweight shell  
**Options:** XS, S, M, L  

### Cairn Packable Rain Shell
`p0085` · **Ƶ 119** · ★ 4.1 (254 reviews) · Stock 14  
⬜ Photography needed  
Emergency weather insurance that disappears into a tail bag until needed. It is cut to pull over armored riding gear, with a helmet-compatible collar and long waterproof zips that are easier to manage with gloves. It packs into its own pocket when the storm passes.  
**Material:** 2.5-layer waterproof nylon · **Protection:** Overshell only; no armor · **Weather:** Waterproof/breathable; taped seams · **Fit:** Sized to layer over riding gear · **Pack Size:** 1.2 L integrated pocket  
**Options:** XS, S, M, L, XL  

### Dustlight Short-Cuff Glove
`p0097` · **Ƶ 69** · ★ 3.7 (33 reviews) · Stock 4  
⬜ Photography needed  
A warm-weather glove with a leather palm where grip and slide protection matter and stretch textile where flexibility matters. The fingers run deliberately shorter than a unisex pattern, reducing the empty fingertips that make small gloves feel clumsy.  
**Material:** Goatskin palm, stretch textile back · **Protection:** CE knuckle protection; reinforced palm · **Weather:** Warm-weather perforation · **Fit:** Women’s short-finger proportions · **Closure:** Single wrist tab  
**Options:** XS, S, M, L  

### Rainline Goreless Touring Glove
`p0109` · **Ƶ 89** · ★ 4.3 (1,487 reviews) · Stock 17  
⬜ Photography needed  
A waterproof glove for cool, wet travel days without the bulk of a winter gauntlet. The palm stays thin enough to feel the controls, while a light lining takes the edge off cold rain. A visor-wiper strip on the left index finger is the tiny feature you miss once you have used one.  
**Material:** Leather/textile hybrid with waterproof membrane · **Protection:** Hard knuckle; palm slider · **Weather:** Waterproof; light insulation · **Fit:** Women’s fit · **Features:** Touchscreen index finger  
**Options:** XS, S, M, L  

### Pebble Technical Trail Glove
`p0121` · **Ƶ 49** · ★ 4.3 (425 reviews) · Stock 16  
⬜ Photography needed  
A close-fitting dirt glove for precise clutch, brake, and switch feel. It is intentionally lighter than a road glove, with a single-layer palm and flexible knuckle overlay rather than bulky hard armor. Best for technical trail sessions where dexterity wins.  
**Material:** Clarino-style palm and ventilated stretch back · **Protection:** Flexible knuckle overlay · **Weather:** Hot/dry trail use · **Fit:** Close technical fit · **Features:** Touchscreen thumb/index  
**Options:** XS, S, M, L  

### Compass 5L Tank Bag
`p0133` · **Ƶ 109** · ★ 3.8 (772 reviews) · Stock 7  
⬜ Photography needed  
Small enough not to dominate the cockpit, large enough for phone, wallet, snacks, power bank, and the things that otherwise migrate through jacket pockets. A cable pass-through supports on-bike charging, and the clear top pocket is intentionally omitted—phones overheat there anyway.  
**Capacity:** 5 L · **Mount:** Magnetic/strap hybrid; non-magnetic strap kit included · **Material:** Ballistic nylon with waterproof cover · **Features:** Glove-friendly zips; cable pass-through · **Dimensions:** 10 × 7 × 5 in  
**Options:** Black, Olive  

### Waypoint 9L Harness Tank Bag
`p0145` · **Ƶ 139** · ★ 4.4 (9 reviews) · Stock 20  
⬜ Photography needed  
A mid-size tank bag for day rides and light travel. The harness stays on the bike while the bag releases with two buckles, and the interior dividers stop small objects from becoming one noisy pile. It expands when the ride unexpectedly acquires pastries.  
**Capacity:** 9 L expandable · **Mount:** Quick-release harness · **Material:** Abrasion-resistant 840D nylon · **Features:** Removable map/tablet sleeve; internal dividers · **Weather:** Water-resistant with seam-taped rain cover  
**Options:** Charcoal, Sand  

### Creekside 12L Tail Pack
`p0157` · **Ƶ 129** · ★ 4.4 (563 reviews) · Stock 20  
⬜ Photography needed  
A waterproof day-ride tail pack sized for layers, lunch, compact tools, and a small first-aid kit. Four anchor straps stay on the bike, so the bag comes off without rethreading everything. The roll-top is less glamorous than a zipper and much less interesting during a downpour.  
**Capacity:** 12 L · **Mount:** Four-point quick-release straps · **Material:** TPU-coated ripstop · **Weather:** Waterproof roll-top main compartment · **Features:** External shock cord; removable shoulder strap  
**Options:** Black, Moss  

### Overnight 22L Tail Duffel
`p0169` · **Ƶ 169** · ★ 4.9 (1,431 reviews) · Stock 14

A one- or two-night motorcycle duffel that keeps its volume manageable on a small ADV bike. The air-purge valve lets soft gear compress after packing, and the wide opening makes it possible to find socks without unpacking the entire trip.  
**Capacity:** 22 L · **Mount:** Four-point webbing system · **Material:** Welded TPU · **Weather:** Waterproof · **Features:** Wide roll-top; air purge valve; shoulder strap  
**Options:** Slate, Clay

### Field Fix ADV Tool Kit
`p0241` · **Ƶ 149** · ★ 4.4 (32 reviews) · Stock 5  
⬜ Photography needed  
A metric roadside kit built around common motorcycle fasteners instead of a generic household assortment. It covers routine adjustments and minor trail repairs without pretending you are rebuilding an engine beside the road.  
**Contents:** 1/4-in ratchet, sockets, bits, combination wrenches, pliers · **Material:** Chrome-vanadium tools · **Weight:** 2.4 lb · **Case:** Compact roll pouch · **Use:** Metric ADV/trail motorcycles  

### Mini Torque T-Handle
`p0253` · **Ƶ 64** · ★ 3.8 (83 reviews) · Stock 23  
⬜ Photography needed  
A small torque tool for the fasteners riders are most tempted to overtighten. The 4–20 Nm range covers many cockpit, bodywork, and light hardware jobs, while the T-handle packs more neatly than a full workshop wrench.  
**Drive:** 1/4 in · **Range:** 4–20 Nm · **Bits:** Hex and Torx set included · **Length:** 6.5 in · **Case:** Slim zip pouch  

### Pocket Trail Pump
`p0265` · **Ƶ 79** · ★ 3.7 (7 reviews) · Stock 3  
⬜ Photography needed  
A genuinely packable compressor for airing back up after a dirt section or dealing with a repaired puncture. It connects directly to a fused SAE lead or battery clamps and includes a backlit gauge, so the job does not require guessing in the dark.
